Chapter 1 Difficult Customer 难缠的顾客

Good evening! My name is Fabio. I'll be your waiter for tonight. May I take your order?

晚上好！我叫法比奥，今晚将为您服务。请问您想点餐了吗？

No, I'm still working on it. This menu's not even in English! What's good here?

还没有，我还在看菜单呢。这菜单连英文都没有！这里有什么好吃的？

For you, sir, I would recommend spaghetti and meatballs.

先生，我建议您试试意大利面配肉丸。

Does it come with Coke and fries?

它会配可乐和薯条吗？

It comes with either soup or salad and a complimentary glass of wine, sir.

它会配汤或沙拉，以及一杯免费的葡萄酒，先生。

I'll go with the spaghetti and meatballs, salad and the wine.

我要意大利面配肉丸、沙拉和葡萄酒。

Excellent choice. Your order will be ready soon.

好极了。您的订单很快就会准备好。

How soon is soon?

多快算快？

20 minutes?

20分钟？

You know what? I'll just go grab a burger across the street.

你知道吗？我就去街对面买个汉堡吃。

Chapter 2 Calling In Sick 打电话请病假

Hello, Daniel speaking, how may I help you?

你好，我是丹尼尔，请问有什么可以帮到您？

Hi Daniel. Julie here.

嗨，丹尼尔，我是朱莉。

Hi Julie, how are you?

嗨，朱莉，你好吗？

Actually, I'm feeling quite ill today.

事实上，我今天感觉不太舒服。

Oh, I'm sorry to hear that. What's wrong?

哦，听到你说这样我很抱歉。怎么了？

I think I'm coming down with the flu. I have a headache, sore throat, a runny nose, and I'm feeling slightly feverish.

我想我可能要得流感了。我头疼，喉咙痛，流鼻涕，而且我感觉有点发烧。

I see… So you're calling in sick?

我明白了。所以你要请病假吗？

Yes, I was hoping to take the day off to recover.

是的，我希望能休一天假来恢复身体。

OK, then. Try and get some rest.

好的，那就好好休息一下吧。


Chapter 3  Daily Life ‐ Hotel Upgrade 酒店升级

Good afternoon. What can I do for you?

下午好，请问有什么需要我帮忙的吗？

I'd like to check in please. I have a reservation under the name Anthony Roberts.

我想办理入住手续，我预订了房间，名字是安东尼·罗伯茨。

All right. Robert. Oh Mr. Roberts. We've been expecting you. And here is your keycard to the Presidential Suite.

好的，罗伯茨，哦，罗伯茨先生，我们一直在等您。这是您总统套房的房卡。

But there must be some mistake. My reservation was for a standard room.

但是一定弄错了，我预订的是标准间。

Are you sure? Let me double check.

您确定吗？让我再确认一下。

Yeah, here. This is my confirmation number.

好的，这是我的确认号码。

You're right Mr. Roberts. There seems to be a mix-up. Unfortunately we're overbooked at the moment.

罗伯茨先生，您说得对。似乎有些混淆。不幸的是，我们现在已经客满了。

So…

所以...

Oh, not to worry. We're pleased to offer you a complimentary upgrade.

哦，别担心。我们很高兴为您提供免费升级。

Woohoo! Presidential Suite, baby!

哇！总统套房，宝贝！

Chapter 4  I need an assistant 我需要一个助手

Like I told you before, we just don't have the resources to hire you an assistant.

就像我之前跟你说过的那样，我们真的没有资源雇佣你一个助理。

I understand that, but the fact is, we're understaffed.

我理解，但事实是，我们人手不足。

The timing is just not right. The economy is bad, and it's too risky to take on new staff.

时机不对。经济不好，现在招新员工风险太大。

Yeah, I guess you're right. Here's an idea. What if we hire an intern? She would take some of the weight off my shoulders.

是啊，我想你是对的。我有个主意，我们雇佣一个实习生怎么样？她可以帮我分担一些压力。

She?

她？

Yeah, you know, a recent graduate. She could give me a hand with some of these projects, and we could keep our costs down.

是啊，你知道的，一个刚毕业的学生。她可以帮我处理一些项目，这样我们就能控制成本。

That sounds reasonable. Let me see what I can do.

这听起来很合理。让我看看我能做些什么。

Tony, I'd like to introduce you to your new assistant.

托尼，我想介绍你认识你的新助理。

Okay, great. Let's meet her.

好的，太好了。我们去见她吧。

Hi, I'm Adam.

你好，我是亚当。

Oh, hi, I'm Tony.

哦，你好，我是托尼。

Chapter 5  Cut In Line 插队

I can't believe it took us two hours to get here. The traffic in New York is unbelievable.

我简直不敢相信我们花了两个小时才到这儿。纽约的交通真是太糟糕了。

Yeah, but just relax honey, we're here and we're going on vacation. In a few hours we'll be in Hawaii and you'll be on the golf course.

是啊，但是放松点，亲爱的，我们已经到了，而且马上就要去度假了。几个小时后我们就到夏威夷了，你就可以去打高尔夫球了。

Oh,no! Look at that line. It must be a mile long. There's no way I'm waiting for another two hours.

哦，不！看看那条队伍，得有一英里长吧。我绝对不可能再等两个小时。

Honey, don't.

亲爱的，别这样。

Hey man, the end of the line is over there. Yeah.

嘿，哥们，队伍的末尾在那儿。

Yeah…

没错。

No, seriously, I was here first and you can't cut in line like this.

不，说真的，我比你先到，你不能这样插队。

Says who?

谁说的？

I do.

我说的。

So sue me!

那你告我好了！

Alright, that's it.

好吧，就这样吧。

Chapter 6 Road Trip 公路旅行

So are we all ready to go?

所以我们都准备好了吗？

Yep, I think so. The car's packed; we have munchies and music and the map's in the car.

是的，我想是的。车已经装满了；我们有零食和音乐，地图也在车里。

Did you get the camera?

你拿相机了吗？

Got it. Did you fill up the tank?

拿到了。你加满油了吗？

Yep, it's all set.

好的，一切都准备就绪了。

You're sure we're not forgetting anything.

你确定我们没有遗漏什么吗？

I'm sure we've got all our bases covered.

我相信我们已经万无一失了。

Well, let's get going then. I love road trips.

那我们出发吧！我超爱公路旅行的。

Um… Do you think we can make a pit stop?

嗯，你觉得我们可以中途停一下吗？

But we've only been on the road for 10 minutes.

可是我们才上路十分钟啊。

I know, but I forgot to go to the bathroom before we left.

我知道，但我忘了出发前去趟洗手间。

Chapter 7 Virus 病毒！

Oh great! The stupid computer froze again. That's the third time today! Hey Samuel, can you come take a look at my PC? It's acting up again. It must have a virus or something.

天啊！这破电脑又死机了，今天已经是第三次了! 嘿，塞缪尔，你能过来看看我的电脑吗？它又出问题了，一定是中了病毒了。

Just give me a second, I'll be right up.

等一下，我马上就来

I ran a virus scan on your computer and it turns out that you have a lot of infected files.

我用杀毒软件扫描了你的电脑，结果发现有很多文件被感染了。

But I'm quite careful when I'm browsing the internet. I have no idea how I could have picked up a virus.

但我上网的时候都很小心啊。 我完全不知道我是怎么感染病毒的。

Well, you have to make sure that your anti-virus software is updated regularly; yours wasn't up to date. That's probably what was causing your problems.

嗯，你必须确保你的杀毒软件定期更新。你的软件没有更新。这可能是导致你出现问题的原因。

Okay, anything else?

好的，还有其他问题吗？

Yeah, try not to kick or hit the computer.

嗯，尽量不要踢或打电脑。

Um yeah, sorry about that.

嗯，抱歉。

Chapter 8  What's your name again 你叫什么名字来着？

Nick, how's it going?

尼克，最近怎么样？

Oh, hey!

哦，你好！

What are you doing in this neighborhood? Do you live around here?

你怎么会在这附近？你住在这附近吗？

Actually, my office is right around the corner.

实际上，我的办公室就在拐角处。

It was great to meet you last week at the conference. I really enjoyed our conversation about foreign investment.

上周在会议上见到你真是太好了。我真的很享受我们关于外国投资的谈话。

Yeah, yeah, it was really interesting. You know, I'm in a bit of a hurry, but here's my card. We should definitely meet up again and continue our discussion.

是啊，是啊，真的很有趣。你知道，我有点赶时间，但这是我的名片。我们一定要再见面，继续我们的讨论。

Sure, you still have my contact details, right?

当然，你还有我的联系方式，对吧？

You know what? This is really embarrassing, but your name has just slipped my mind. Can you remind me?

你知道吗？这真的很尴尬， 但我就是想不起你的名字了。你能提醒我一下吗？

Sure. My name is Anna Ferris. Don't worry about it. It happens to me all the time. I'm terrible with names too.

当然。我叫安娜·费里斯。别担心，我也经常这样。我也记不住名字。

Chapter 9 Silence please! 请安静

Those people in front of us are making so much noise. It's so inconsiderate.

前面那些人太吵了，真是太不考虑别人了。

Don't worry about it. It's not such a big deal.

别担心，没什么大不了的。

Oh, I can't hear a thing. Excuse me. Can you keep it down?

哦，我什么也听不见。不好意思，能小声点吗？

Sure. Sorry about that.

好的，抱歉。

Someone's phone is ringing.

有人电话响了。

Honey, I think it's your phone. Did you forget to switch it off?

亲爱的，我觉得是你的手机。你忘记关机了吗？

Oh no. You're right. That's so embarrassing.

哦，不。你说得对。真尴尬。

Do you mind keeping it down? I'm trying to watch a movie here.

你能把声音调低一点吗？我正在这里看电影。

Chapter 10  Driving Sales 推动销售

All right people, we're holding this meeting today because we've got to do something about our sales and we need to do it now. I want concrete solutions. How do you plan to drive sales? Roger.

好了各位，我们今天开会是因为我们必须采取行动解决销售问题，而且现在就需要行动。我想要具体的解决方案。你打算如何推动销售？罗杰。

Well, uh, in fact we're the most expensive in the market, so maybe we need to lower our prices to match the competitors.

嗯，事实上，我们是市场上最贵的，所以也许我们需要降低价格以与竞争对手保持一致.

Lower our prices? Not very creative. It'll never fly with Swan. What kind of thinking is that? Geez. Anybody else have a better plan? Natalie.

降低价格？一点都不创意。斯旺绝对不会同意。这是什么想法？天哪。还有其他人有更好的计划吗？娜塔莉。

Um, perhaps, um, a promotion. Maybe a two-for-one offer, or something like that.

嗯，也许，嗯，一个促销活动。也许是买一送一的优惠，或者类似的东西。

What? That's the same thing. Bad idea. Really bad idea. Dammit people come on! Think! The CEO will be here any minute.

什么？那是一样的。糟糕的主意。非常糟糕的主意。该死的人们，振作起来！想想！CEO马上就要到了。

Do we have any ideas yet?

我们有想法了吗？

Yes, Mr. Swan. We were kind of considering a two-for-one offer to get more competitive.

是的，斯旺先生。我们正在考虑一个买一送一的优惠，以提高竞争力。

A two-for-one promotion? Hmm, I kind of like the sound of that. It sounds like something we should consider.

买一送一的促销活动？嗯，我挺喜欢这个想法的。听起来像是我们应该考虑的事情。

Yeah, exactly. Just what I was thinking. In fact, that's a brilliant idea. I'm glad we thought of that. Very creative.

没错，我也有同样的想法。事实上，这真是个绝妙的主意。我很高兴我们想到了这一点，非常有创意。

Chapter 11 New Guy in Town 镇上的新来者

Oh, I don't know if you heard, but someone moved into that old house down the road.

哦，我不知道你是否听说过，但有人搬进了那条路尽头的旧房子。

Yeah, I know. I met the owner of the house yesterday as he was moving in.His name is Armand.

是的，我知道。昨天我遇到房子的主人，当时他正在搬进来。他叫阿尔芒。

Really? What's he like? You have to fill me in.

真的吗？他怎么样？你得告诉我详细情况。

Actually, he's a bit strange. I don't know. I've just got a bad feeling about him.

说真的，他有点奇怪。我不知道，我就是对他感觉不太好。

Really? Why?

真的吗？为什么？

Well, yesterday I brought over a housewarming gift, but Armand started acting really weird, and then he practically kicked me out.I tried to sort of peek into his house, but everything was so dark inside that I couldn't really get a good look.The whole thing really creeped me out.

嗯，昨天我带了份乔迁礼物过去，但是阿尔芒开始表现得很奇怪，然后他几乎把我赶走了。我试图往他房子里偷看，但是里面太黑了，我什么也看不清。这整件事真的让我毛骨悚然。

Well, you'll never guess what I saw this morning.A delivery truck pulled into his driveway, and it dropped off a long, rectangular box.It almost looked like a coffin.

好吧，你永远猜不到我今天早上看到了什么。一辆送货卡车驶进了他的车道，然后放下了一个长方形的盒子。它几乎看起来像一口棺材。

You see? Why would he…

你明白吗？他为什么要...

Hello ladies…

你好！女士们~

Ah, Armand! Oh, you scared the heck out of me. This is my friend Doris.

啊，阿尔芒，哦，你吓死我了！这位是我的朋友多丽丝。

A pleasure to meet you. If you're not doing anything tonight, I'd like to have you both for dinner. I mean, I'd like to have you both over for dinner.

很高兴认识你。如果你们今晚没事，我想邀请你们俩一起共进晚餐。我的意思是，我想邀请你们俩来我家共进晚餐。

Chapter 12 Cleaning the House 打扫房子

Honey, the house is such a mess! I need you to help me tidy up a bit. My boss and her husband are coming over for dinner and the house needs to be spotless.

亲爱的，家里乱成一团糟！我需要你帮我整理一下。我的老板和她的丈夫要来吃饭，家里必须一尘不染。

I'm in the middle of something right now. I'll be there in a second.

我现在正忙着呢。我马上就来。

This can't wait! I need your help now.

等不了了！我现在需要你的帮忙。

Alright, alright, I'm coming.

好了，好了，我来了。

Okay. Here's a list of chores we need to get done. I'll do the dishes, and get all the groceries for tonight. You can sweep and mop the floors.Oh, and the furniture needs to be dusted.

好的。这是我们需要完成的家务清单。我会洗碗，然后去买今晚的食材。你可以扫地和拖地。哦，还有家具需要除尘。

You know what? I have to pick something up at the mall. So why don't you clean the floors and I'll go to the supermarket and get the groceries.

你知道吗？我得去商场买点东西。那你就先打扫一下地板吧，我去超市买点菜。

Sure, that's fine. Here's the list of all the things you need to get. Don't forget anything, and can you pick up a bottle of wine on your way home?

好的，没问题。这是你需要买的东西清单。别忘了什么，回家的时候能顺路买瓶酒吗？

Hey, honey I'm back. Wow, the house looks really good.

亲爱的，我回来了。哇，这房子看起来真不错。

Great. Can you set the table.

太好了。你能摆一下桌子吗？

Just a sec, I'm just gonna vacuum this rug real fast.

等一下，我马上把地毯吸一下。

Wait, don't turn it on!

等等，别开它！

Chapter 13  Out Of Control Spending 失控的开支

Okay, so now the last point on our agenda.Jill, let's go over the profit and loss statement.

好的，现在我们议程的最后一个议题。吉尔，我们一起看一下损益表。

Great! The main issue here, as you can see, is that our expenses are through the roof!

太好了！正如你所见，这里的主要问题是我们的支出已经高得离谱了！

Let's see. These numbers are off the charts. What's going on here!

我们来看看。这些数字已经超出了图表范围。这到底是怎么回事！

Well umm, sir, the company expenditures on entertainment and travel are out of control. Look at these bills for example just this month we've paid over twenty thousand dollars for hotel charges!

嗯，先生，公司在娱乐和旅行方面的支出已经失控了。看看这些账单，就拿这个月来说，我们已经支付了超过2万美元的酒店费用！

Okay. Thank you. I'll look into it.

好的，谢谢。我会调查这件事的。

The list goes on and on. Here this is a bill for five thousand dollars for spa treatment.

这还只是冰山一角，这里还有一张五千美元的温泉疗养账单。

Thank you. That will be all. I'll take care of it.

谢谢，就这些了，我会处理好的。

Look at this one sir, eight thousand dollars were spent in one night at a place called "Wild Things"?!

先生，您看看这个，一晚上就在一个叫“狂野之物”的地方花了8000美元？

Okay. I get it. Thank you for your very thorough analysis!

好的，我明白了。感谢您如此详细的分析！

Chapter 14 I’m in Debt 我负债了

Hello, I'm here to see Mr. Corleone.

您好，我来见科里昂先生。

Right this way, sir.

这边请，先生。

Charlie, what can I do for you?

查理，我能为你做些什么？

Mr. Corlone I'm really sorry to trouble you, but I need your help.

科里昂先生，我真的很抱歉打扰您，但我需要您的帮助。

Anything for you Charlie! Your father was like a brother to me.

查理，为了你，什么都行！你父亲对我来说就像兄弟一样。

Well sir, you see this recession has hit me pretty hard, But I lost my job and I'm in a lot of debt.

先生，您看，这经济衰退对我打击很大，我丢了工作，现在负债累累。

I see.

我明白了。

Yeah, you know… I've got credit card bills, car payments, I've got to pay my mortgage; and on top of all that, I have to pay my son's college tuition.

是啊，你知道的… 我有信用卡账单要还，还有车贷，房贷也要还！ 更糟糕的是，我还得支付儿子的大学学费。

So you're asking for a loan.

所以你是想借钱？

Well, I just thought maybe you could help me out.

我只是觉得也许你能帮帮我。

What? At a time like this, I'm broke too, you know. You're not the only one who has been hit by the recession.I lost half my money in the stock market crash. Go on, get outa here.

什么？这种时候我也很穷，你知道的。你不是唯一一个受到经济衰退影响的人。我在股市崩盘中损失了一半的钱。走吧，赶快离开这里吧。

Chapter 15 I’m sorry, I love you 对不起，我爱你

Whoa! What's going on? Watch out!

哇！发生什么事了？小心点！

Hey! Watch where you're going!

嘿！注意你的去向！

Oh no! I'm so sorry! Are you alright?

哦，不！我真的很抱歉！你还好吗？

Oh…I don'tknow.

哦，我不知道。

I feel terrible. I really didn't mean to knock you over. My tire just exploded and I lost control of my bike.Really, it was an accident. Please accept my apologies.

我感觉很糟糕，我真的不是故意撞到你的。我的轮胎突然爆了，我失去了对自行车的控制。真的，这只是一场意外。请接受我的道歉。

Just let me try to stand up.

让我试着站起来吧。

Oh, wait a second. You seem really familiar. I think I know you from somewhere.

哦，等等，你看起来很眼熟。我想我以前见过你。

Yeah, that's right.I think we've met somewhere before.We met at Aaron's place last weekend.What a coincidence. But anyway, I'm glad to see you're not badly hurt, and I should probably get going. I've got a nine o 'clock meeting. But here's my number. Call me and we'll exchange insurance information.

没错，就是你。我想我们以前见过面。我们上周末在艾伦家见过面。真是太巧了。不过话说回来，我很高兴看到你没有严重受伤，我应该走了。我九点有个会议。这是我的号码。给我打电话，我们交换一下保险信息。

Ouch! My ankle! I think it's broken. You can't just leave me like this.

哎哟！我的脚踝！我感觉它断了。你不能就这么把我丢在这里。

Are you calling an ambulance?

你是在叫救护车吗？

Nope, I'm cancelling my appointment so that I can stay here with you.

不，我取消了我的预约，这样我就能留在这里陪着你。

Chapter 16 Turn left here! 在这里左转

Hurry up, get in!

快点，上车！

I'm in, let's go.

我上来了，走吧。

Okay, make a left here. No wait, I meant make a right! Come on, speed up!

好的，这里左转。 等等，我是说右转！快点，加速！

Jeez, what's the rush?

天哪，这么着急干什么？

Don't worry about it. Just drive. Oh no, the light is about to change.Step on it.

别担心了。开车吧。哎哟，红绿灯要变色了。快踩油门！

Are you nuts? I'm not going to run a red light.

你疯了吗？我不会闯红灯的。

Whatever. Just turn right here.The freeway will be packed at this hour.Let's take a side street.Go on. Get out of our way. Move! Move!

随便吧，就在这里右转。在这个时间段，高速公路肯定挤满了车。咱们走条小路吧。快走开，别挡着我们。快走！快走！

What's your problem? Jeez, having a fit is not going to help.

你到底怎么了？天哪，发脾气解决不了问题。

这边我知道一条捷径。直接从这里下去，我们就能穿过阿什伯恩高地。走吧！快走！小心那个女士。

I'm going as fast as I can.

我已经在尽力赶路了。

太好了，我们赶上了。五点五十八分，就在图书馆关门前。

You're such a geek.

你真是个书呆子。

Chapter 18 Protest! 抗议！

Thousands of angry citizens are protesting against the proposed bailout of the auto manufacturing industry.

数千名愤怒的市民正在抗议拟议中的汽车制造业救助计划。

先生，先生，我是莎拉·奥康奈尔，行动5新闻报道，您能告诉我们发生了什么事吗？

Yeah, yeah. We're here because we feel this is an injustice. The financial irresponsibility of big business has to stop.

没错，没错。我们来这里是因为我们觉得这是不公平的。大企业的财务不负责任必须停止。

我们来这里是为了向政府表明我们不喜欢他们花纳税人的钱的方式。

先生，但究竟是什么让每个人如此愤怒？

It's an absolute outrage, Sarah. The U.S. government wants to give 25 billion of taxpayers' money to the auto industry.

这简直是不可理喻，莎拉。美国政府想要拨款250亿美元的纳税人资金给汽车行业。

These are companies that have been mismanaged and are now nearly bankrupt.

这些公司管理不善，现在几乎濒临破产。

I see. But many supporters of the bailout argue that it could help save the jobs of millions of hard-working Americans.

我明白。但是许多支持救助的人认为，这可以帮助拯救数百万勤劳的美国人的工作。

That may be true. And I, for one, don't want to see anyone lose their job.

这可能是真的。我个人也不想看到任何人失去工作。

But how can these CEOs ask for a bailout when they're making millions of dollars? And then they have the nerve to fly to Washington in private jets.

但是，当这些首席执行官赚取数百万美元时，他们怎么能要求救助呢？然后他们竟然厚颜无耻地乘坐私人飞机飞往华盛顿。

This costs hundreds of thousands of dollars. They're asking for money. That is just not right.

这花费了数十万美元。他们正在寻求资金。这简直是不可理喻。

Good point. This is Sarah O 'Connell reporting live from Washington, D .C. Back to you, Tom.

说得对。这里是来自华盛顿特区的莎拉·奥康奈尔现场报道。轮到你了，汤姆。

先生，你被捕了，你有权保持沉默，你最好别撅嘴，最好别哭，你所说的一切都将成为呈堂证供，你有权请律师，如果你请不起，法庭会为你指定一位。

嘿，医生！我觉得我可能需要配眼镜了。最近老是头疼，看远处的东西特别费劲。可我一直以来视力都是1.5的。

听起来你可能有点远视。好的，那么请遮住你的左眼，然后读出你面前的视力表。

嗯，X.E.R. 3，一个问号，另一个符号我看不太清楚，但我想那是和平符号。

Arthur, you're as blind as a bat.

亚瑟，你简直跟蝙蝠一样瞎。

是的，我知道，我的视力有时真的很模糊。

好的，那你去另一个房间挑选镜框吧，我来填写你的处方。

谢谢医生！

Chapter 25 Planning For The Worst 做最坏的打算

Well right, let's move to our next order of business.

好的，让我们进入下一个议程。

As many of you are aware, in recent weeks there has been a lot of media coverage surrounding this bird flu issue, and it's come to my attention that our company lacks any sort of bird flu contingency plan.

正如大家所知，最近几周，媒体对禽流感问题的报道很多，我注意到我们公司还没有任何禽流感应急计划。

Basically, we need to come up with a clear plan.

基本上，我们需要制定一个明确的计划。

We need to outline specific actions that our company can take to maintain critical business functions in case a pandemic strikes.

我们需要概述公司在疫情爆发的情况下，为维持关键业务功能可以采取的具体行动。

So what I'd like to do is: First appoint someone to look after drafting our plan.

所以，我想做的第一件事是：指派一个人负责起草我们的计划。

Ralph, I'd like you to head up this project.

拉尔夫，我希望你能负责这个项目。

Sure. No problem. What issues do you want me to consider?

好的，没问题。你想让我考虑哪些问题？

Well, let's see. There are a few points we need to be thinking about.

嗯，让我们看看。有一些要点我们需要考虑。

First, I'll need you to analyze our numbers and figure out what kind of financial impact an outbreak might have.

首先，我需要你分析我们的数据，并弄清楚疫情爆发可能带来的财务影响。

You will also need to think about how we can avoid any of our employees getting infected.

我们还需要考虑如何避免我们的员工感染。

Think of ways to reduce employee customer contact, perhaps some IT solutions that will allow our people to work from home.

想想如何减少员工与客户的接触，也许一些IT解决方案可以让我们员工在家工作。

I guess you'll need me to forecast employee absences as well, right?

我想你也需要我预测员工的缺勤情况，对吧？

And I'll think about the impact this will have on our clients.

我会考虑这对我们客户的影响。

Hey, what about vacines? Should we be thinking about getting vaccines for our employees?

嘿，疫苗怎么样？我们是否应该考虑为员工接种疫苗？

Exactly right. So I'll leave this to you, and we'll review the draft plan in two weeks, Ok so anyone want to order some KFC for lunch?

没错，那就交给你了，两周后我们再一起看看草案，好，那有人想点肯德基当午餐吗？

Chapter 29 Investing in Emerging Markets 投资新兴市场

Dad, I'd like to borrow some money!

爸爸，我想借点钱！

Sure, Johnny. How much do you need?

当然可以，约翰尼。你需要多少？

Five bucks? Come on Dad, I need 30 ,000.

五块钱？拜托，爸爸，我需要30,000。

I wanna get into the market.

我想进军市场。

You know, I'm tired of hearing all this news about the economic downturn, the inevitable recession, people stuffing their money in their mattresses.

你知道吗，我厌倦了听到所有关于经济衰退、不可避免的经济衰退以及人们把钱塞进床垫里的新闻。

I look at this as an opportunity!

我认为这是一个机会！

This is a chance for me to get a jump start on building my Nest Egg.

这是一个让我开始积累养老金的机会。

I don't know about that, with all the uncertainty in the markets right now it would be a very unwise decision to invest.

我不确定，现在市场充满了不确定性，投资将是一个非常不明智的决定。

I don't know if you are aware son, but there has been a lot of turmoil in the markets recently.

我不知道你是否知道，儿子，最近市场动荡很大。

There have already been half a million layoffs in the last few months and we have no idea how the proposed stimulus package will impact the economy.

过去几个月已经发生了五十万人的裁员，我们也无法预知拟议的刺激方案会对经济产生怎样的影响。

There's just too much instability.

现在局势实在太不稳定了。

I just wouldn't feel comfortable investing in this climate.

在这种情况下，我实在没有信心投资。

But look at it this way, every challenge is an opportunity.

但换个角度想，每一次挑战都是机遇。

仅此一项，对消费品的总需求就代表着一个巨大的财富创造机会。

得了吧，孩子，你对这件事看得太天真了。

就像我说的，我拥有一栋房子，你看，这是我的银行对账单，显示了我的投资和银行余额。对不起，先生，这次我们无法为您提供B2签证延期，但您获得了居民签证，恭喜您，您是第百万个申请签证的人，您赢了，恭喜您

Chapter 47 Just In Time Strategy 准时制策略

I called this meeting today in order to discuss our manufacturing plan, as I'm sure you're all aware, with the credit crunch and the global financial crisis.

我今天召集大家开会，是为了讨论我们的生产计划，正如大家所知，当前正值信贷紧缩和全球金融危机时期。

We're obligated to look for more cost -efficient ways of producing our goods.

我们有义务寻找更具成本效益的方式来生产我们的产品。

We don't want to have to be looking at redundancies, so we've outlined a brief plan to implement the just-in-time philosophy.

我们不想被迫裁员，因此我们制定了一个简要计划，以实施准时制理念。

We have two basic points that we want to focus on.

我们有两个基本要点需要重点关注。

First of all, we want to reduce our lead time.

首先，我们要缩短我们的交付周期。

Why would we want to do that?

为什么要这样做呢？

I think this is not an area that really needs to be worked on.

我认为这个想法并不需要过多讨论。

Well, we want to reduce production and delivery lead times for better overall efficiency.

嗯，我们要缩短生产和交付周期，以提高整体效率。

Right! Production lead times can be reduced by moving workstations closer together… …reducing queue length like, for example, reducing the number of jobs waiting to be processed at a given machine… and improving the coordination and cooperation between successive processes.

没错！生产周期可以通过将工作站移近来缩短……比如，减少排队长度，例如减少在特定机器上等待处理的作业数量……以及改善后续流程之间的协调与合作。

Delivery lead times can be reduced through close cooperation with suppliers, possibly by inducing suppliers to locate closer to the factory or working with a faster shipping company.

通过与供应商密切合作，可以缩短交货时间，例如鼓励供应商将工厂设在离我们更近的地方，或者与更快的运输公司合作。

I see. That makes sense.

我明白了，这很有道理。

The second point is that we want to require supplier quality assurance and implement a zero defects quality program.

第二点是我们希望要求供应商进行质量保证，并实施零缺陷质量计划。

We currently have far too many errors that lead to defective items and therefore they must be eliminated a quality control at the source program must be implemented to give workers personal responsibility for the quality of the work they do and the authority to stop production when something goes wrong.

我们目前存在太多导致产品缺陷的错误，因此必须消除这些错误，必须实施源头质量控制计划，赋予员工对工作质量的个人责任，并赋予他们当出现问题时停止生产的权力。

I'm with you on this one. It's essential that you reduce these errors we've got to force our suppliers to reduce that mistake. Exactly. Well, let's look at how we're going to put this plan into action. First…

我正在等待这个，减少这些错误至关重要，我们必须迫使我们的供应商减少这些错误，没错，让我们先看看如何将这个计划付诸行动。

Chapter 52 Pizza Delivary 披萨外送

Now, I have a couple of final questions.

I hope they're not too hard.

我希望它们不要太难。

嗯，我们为什么要雇佣你呢？

你知道，我在建立牢固的长期消费者关系方面非常出色。

我决定我们需要对我们的产品进行更充分的信息和技术准备。

因此，在与我们技术部门协调后，我实施了一系列培训课程。

We were able to solve 90 % of our customers' issues.

我们成功解决了 90% 的客户问题。

如果给我机会，我能为这家公司带来这种成功。

Woo! Impressive. So, what would you consider to be your greatest weakness?

哇！真厉害。那么，你认为你最大的弱点是什么？

To be honest, I struggle with organization and time management.

说实话，我很难做到条理分明，也很难管理好时间。

Punctuality has never been a strength of mine.

我从来就不是一个守时的人。

I find it hard to organize my time effectively.

我发现有效地安排时间对我来说很难。

I've actually addressed this weakness recently by attending a workshop on efficient time management.

我最近通过参加一个关于高效时间管理的研讨会来解决这个弱点。

It helped me a lot by providing me with great insights on how to get organized and use my time efficiently, so I think I'm getting better now.

它给了我很多帮助，让我对如何整理和高效利用时间有了深刻的见解，所以我觉得我现在正在进步。

Great. Well, let me tell you that I am very pleased with this interview.

很好，让我告诉你，我对这次面试非常满意。

我们这周正在筛选候选人。

下周，我们将通知入围候选人与我们首席执行官进行第二次面试的日期和时间。

Great. Thanks a lot.

太好了，非常感谢。

我希望收到你的消息。

Goodbye.

再见。

Chapter 66 Calling The Office

A: Ello-hay, Aniel-day eaking-spay, ow-hay ay-may I elp-hay ou-yay 
B: Ay-hay, Aniel-day, Ulie-jay ere-hay 
A: Ay-hay, Ulie-jay, ow-hay are ou-yay? 
B: Actually, Im eeling-fay ite-quay ill otday-tay. 
A: Im orry-say oo-tay ear-hay, at-they. ut-way is ong-wray? 
B: I ink-thay Im oming-cay own-day ith-way uhthey oo-flay. I ave-hay a eadache-hay, a ore-say oat-thray and Im eeling-fay ighly-slay everishfay. 
A: I ee-say. O-say oure-yay alling-cay in ick-say? 
B: Es-yay, I uz-way oping-hay oo-tay ake-tay uh-they ay-day off oo-tay eecover-ray. 
A: Ok, en-they. Ay-tray and et-gay ome-say est-ray.

这是一种叫做“猪拉丁语”（Pig Latin）的语言游戏。它通过将单词的首字母或首音节移到单词的末尾，然后加上“ay”来变换原单词。比如“hello”变成“ello-hay”，“how”变成“ow-hay”。这种语言形式通常用于玩笑和非正式的对话，目的是让话听起来更有趣或者难以理解。

我们有必要理解它们是如何形成的，以及形成的原因。

Does anyone know what we celebrate on May 1st?

那我们放假一天吗？

No, it is not considered to be a national holiday here in the u .s.

不，在美国这里它不被认为是法定假日。

But in other countries it is oh man in the 19th century working conditions were appalling, with workers being forced to work 10, 12 and 14 hours a day.

然而，在其他国家，19 世纪的劳动条件非常糟糕，工人被迫每天工作 10、12 甚至 14 个小时。

Support for the 8 -hour workday movement was growing rapidly, despite the indifference and hostility of many union leaders.

尽管许多工会领导人漠不关心甚至敌视，但支持八小时工作制运动的呼声正在迅速增长。

And by April 1886, 250 ,000 workers were involved in the May Day Movement.

到1886年4月，已有25万名工人参与了五一运动。

Previous legislative attempts to improve working conditions had failed so labor organizers took drastic measures.

此前改善工作条件的立法尝试都失败了，因此工会组织者采取了极端的措施。

They passed the resolutions stating that 8 hours would constitute a legal day's work and on May 1st 1886 the resolution took effect cool Is that why we only work eight hours now? Yes, but the happy ending came at a high price on May 3rd 1886 police fired into a crowd of strikers at the McCormick Reaper works factory Killing four and wounding many a Mass meeting

他们通过了决议，规定8小时为一天的合法工作时间，并在1886年5月1日生效。所以我们现在只工作8小时吗？是的，但这个圆满结局的代价很高。1886年5月3日，警察向麦考密克收割机工厂的罢工人群开枪，造成4人死亡，多人受伤，引发了一场大规模集会。

was called for the next day to protest the brutality and then what happened well As we say, the rest is history.

人们号召第二天举行抗议活动，以谴责暴行，然后发生了什么？正如我们所说，剩下的都是历史了。

对不起，先生。航空公司规定每位乘客最多可托运两件行李，每件重量不超过20公斤，此外还可以携带一件手提行李。

That bag is clearly too big.

Chapter 94 Big Bang Theory		宇宙大爆炸理论

你怎么了？看起来不太好。是啊，头疼，就这些了。

It's killer. I liked physics.

这真是要命，我喜欢物理。

It's all math really, ARCS, curves, velocity, cool stuff Yeah, but today's lesson was all about the creation of the universe. A physics class about the creation of the universe That's some pretty unscientific language there sounds more religious to me. It's all religion.

这其实都是数学，弧线、曲线、速度，很酷的东西。是的，但今天的课全是关于宇宙的创造。一节关于宇宙创造的物理课，那听起来有点不科学，更像是宗教。这全是宗教。

Take the theory of the Big Bang.

以宇宙大爆炸理论为例。

How is it possible that all of the stuff in universe comes from an explosion?

宇宙中的所有物质，怎么可能都来自于一次爆炸呢？

That's no better than Atlus carrying the globe on his back or African myths about turtles and stuff.

这并不比阿特拉斯背负地球，或者非洲神话中关于乌龟之类的传说，好到哪里去。

Turtles? Whatever. Look, all that's required for the creation of matter is an imbalance of particles and antiparticles, at least that's what the math says.

乌龟？随便吧。听着，物质的产生只需要粒子与反粒子的不平衡，至少数学上是这么说的。

Math schmath. What's the evidence?

数学算什么。证据在哪？

There is evidence. You know Edwin Hubble? The guy who, in the early 20th century, was the first scientist to measure the drift of matter in the universe, thus advancing notions of an expanding universe.

有证据。你认识埃德温·哈勃吗？他在20世纪初期，是第一个测量宇宙物质漂移的科学家，从而推动了宇宙膨胀的概念。

What would it be expanding from?

它将从哪里扩展？

Well, the big bang, duh.

好吧，就是宇宙大爆炸，这还用说吗？

Anyway it's just a theory.

无论如何，这只是一个理论。

Why do people go around touting theories?

为什么人们会到处宣扬理论？

Where's the rigor in that?

那里面哪里有严谨性呢？

Dude, don't equivocate.

哥们，别跟我打马虎眼。

一种理论，只有在经受住严格的检验之后，才能成为理论。

你又让我头疼了。

Chapter 99 Movie Review		影评

Welcome back movie lovers to another premiere movie review.

欢迎回来，影迷们，又一场首映电影评论开始了。

My name is Richard Clark and I am joined today by the very erudite David Watson.

我的名字是理查德·克拉克，今天与我一同出席的，是学识渊博的大卫·沃森。

Thank you, Dick. Today, we are going to talk about the movie Lion King.

谢谢你，迪克。今天，我们要来聊聊电影《狮子王》。

Tell me, what is your impression of this film?

告诉我，你对这部电影的印象是什么？

I think this film is simply a fable depicting man's eternal greed for power, and in my opinion, it's a very fine film. Even despite the accusations of plagiarism of traditional folk tales from other countries, the musical score was amazing.

我认为，这部电影，不过是一则寓言，描绘了人类对权力的永恒贪婪，在我看来，这是一部非常出色的电影。即便被指控抄袭了其他国家的传统民间故事，其配乐依然令人惊叹。

The animation was very well done, and the story was simply enchanting.

动画制作得非常出色，故事情节也相当迷人。

I think otherwise. Even though the animation was technically strong, and as you say, the score and songs performed by Elton John were great, the film lacks a certain originality.

我持不同看法，尽管动画在技术上很出色，而且如你所说，埃尔顿·约翰的配乐和歌曲都很棒，但这部电影缺乏一定的原创性。

It lacked heart, and I would dare to say that it was too predictable.

它缺乏灵魂，我敢说，它太过可预测了。

Predictable??? How???

可预测？！？！怎么预测？！？！

Come on Dave, it's a G -rated movie.

来吧，戴夫，这是一部适合所有年龄段的电影。

It's for the kids. It's not a thriller.

这是给孩子们看的，不是惊悚片。

Well that's just it, it did have some very dramatic and intense scenes, for example, when Mufasa dies, or the dark, grim portrayal of Scar.

Chapter 102 Global Warming 全球变暖

And therefore global warming is the greatest deception of the early 21st century.

Questions?

Well, yes. Essentially, the historical record supports a theory of climate cycles.

嗯，是的。本质上，历史记录支持气候循环的理论。

Warming and cooling are cooperating processes in the planetary ecosystem.

变暖与变冷，是行星生态系统中相互协作的过程。

If that's true and the planet is getting cooler, what explains the rapid melting of the polar ice caps and the dramatic rise in the global average temperatures?

如果这是真的，地球正在变冷，那么如何解释极地冰盖的快速融化，以及全球平均气温的急剧上升呢？

But are global temperatures rising? If you look at the data from 1975, you'll be misled.

但是，全球气温在上升吗？如果你只看1975年的数据，你可能会被误导。

If you were serious, you would look at the record starting in the 1880s. Then you would see how dramatically the Earth's temperature has changed.

如果你认真对待，你会查看从19世纪80年代开始的记录。然后，你会看到地球温度发生了多么显著的变化。

Young lady, I'd beg to differ. Look, the point of the lecture was to emphasize that there's evidence for both sides.

小姐，我不得不表示异议，你看，这次讲座的重点在于强调，双方的观点都有证据支持。

And I'm putting forth the argument that there's greater evidence in support of the global cooling hypothesis. Look, it's an indisputable fact that the public is being manipulated and scared into believing that there's some kind of climate crisis.

我提出的论点是，有更多的证据支持全球变冷的假说。看吧，公众被操纵和恐吓，让他们相信存在某种气候危机，这是一个无可争辩的事实。

This scare mongering is done quite simply for political reasons.

这种恐吓行为，纯粹是出于政治原因。

但即便不考虑二氧化碳等温室气体导致全球变暖这一令人不安的现实，这个话题难道不正是政客们应该讨论的吗？

Not if they want to use your tax dollars and mine to fund completely unnecessary initiatives.

如果他们想用你我的税款，来资助完全不必要的项目，那就不行。

Yeah, like conservation, protecting endangered species and investing in renewable energy.

是的，比如保护环境，保护濒危物种，以及投资可再生能源。

至少，你得承认，这场辩论有可能终结我们对进口石油的依赖。

Buying oil supports autocratic countries that use these revenues to devastating ends.

购买石油，支持了那些专制国家，这些国家利用石油收入，造成了毁灭性的后果。

在我所有的日子里，我从未受到过如此不尊重的对待。

I'm a professor, a scientist, and a researcher of high regard.

我是一位教授，一位科学家，也是一位备受尊敬的研究者。

是啊，而且还是个两面派。大家都知道，你是石油游说团体的代言人。

Chapter 108  Bad News, Boss! 坏消息，老板！

A: ...Now that we have been over the gory details of our disastrous first quarter, Ed, give us some good news. How are things looking for us in terms of sales this month?  

A：……既然我们已经了解了第一季度惨不忍睹的具体情况，埃德，说点好消息吧。我们这个月的销售情况怎么样？

B: Uh, well... Would you like the bad news first or the really bad news?  

B：呃，这个……您想先听坏消息，还是特别糟糕的消息？

A: What? Ed, don’t tell me you only have bad news!  

A：什么？埃德，别告诉我你带来的全是坏消息！

B: Well, sir, our sales have dropped—no, plunged—fifty percent in the past month alone. We are currently overstocked and overstaffed, and our profits are falling fast. The market is in recession, and we have no way of moving our inventory or getting rid of our staff. If we consider redundancies, it would cost us a fortune because of the new regulations governing compensation packages. It’s a real mess.  

B：是这样的，先生。仅仅在过去一个月，我们的销售额就下降了——不，应该说暴跌了——百分之五十。目前，我们库存积压、人员过剩，利润也在迅速下滑。市场正处于衰退期，我们既无法清理库存，也没办法裁减员工。由于新出台的遣散补偿规定，如果考虑裁员，我们将不得不支付一大笔费用。现在真是一团糟。

A: For crying out loud... How fast are we losing money?  
A：真是受够了……我们亏损的速度到底有多快？

B: Um... How can I put this? Let’s just say that at this pace, we will be filing for Chapter 11 in less than three months.  

B：嗯……该怎么说呢？这么说吧，照这个速度下去，不到三个月，我们就得申请破产保护了。

A: What? Geez! How could this have happened? So, what’s the bad news?  

A：什么？天哪！事情怎么会变成这样？那么，坏消息又是什么？

B: Oh, that’s the really bad news. Our supplier suffered QC problems, and, well, half of our production is faulty. We’re going to have to recall all items sold in the last quarter. And the worst part? We’re going to have to shoulder this cost.  

B：哦，接下来才是真正糟糕的消息。我们的供应商在质量控制方面出了问题，结果……我们有一半的产品存在缺陷。我们不得不召回上一季度售出的全部产品。最糟糕的是，这笔费用得由我们承担。

A: Are you joking? Get the supplier on the line now! They have to assume the costs of this mess!  

A：你在开玩笑吗？马上接通供应商的电话！这场烂摊子造成的损失必须由他们承担！

B: We tried that, sir. The factory has gone under, and the owner apparently has fled the country.  

：我们已经试过了，先生。那家工厂已经倒闭了，而且老板显然已经逃往国外。

A：我们完蛋了！

B：不过，倒是有一个特别好的消息！

A：真的吗？什么好消息？

B: I got offered a new job!

Chapter 109 Breaking Up  分手

B：什么？为什么？我是说，我们的感情虽然有过起伏，偶尔也会产生分歧，但我们在一起很幸福，不是吗？

A: That’s just it. I’m not happy anymore, Tim. It’s not you; it’s me. I know that I can be hard to deal with, and you are a great guy! You are the type of guy that any woman would kill for!  

A：问题就在这里，蒂姆，我已经不再感到幸福了。不是你的问题，是我的问题。我知道自己有时很难相处，而你是个非常优秀的男人！你是任何女人都梦寐以求的那种男人！

B: So, what are you saying? You’re breaking up with me because I’m perfect?  

B：那么，你到底是什么意思？你要和我分手，是因为我太完美了吗？

A: Tim, you are too good for me. You deserve someone who can make you smile and make you happy the way that you made me happy. Oh, I could say that I’ll be all you need, but that would be a lie. I know I’d only hurt you. I know I’d only make you cry.  

A：蒂姆，你对我来说太好了。你值得拥有一个能让你微笑、让你幸福的人，就像你曾经带给我幸福一样。我也可以说，我会成为你需要的一切，但那只是谎言。我知道自己只会伤害你，只会让你流泪。

B: Baby, come on. Don’t do this to me! Whatever it is, we can work it out. Just give me another chance! I know that we can get through this, but we’ve got to stick together! Don’t leave me.  

B：宝贝，别这样。不要这样对我！无论出了什么问题，我们都可以一起解决。再给我一次机会吧！我知道我们可以渡过这个难关，但我们必须在一起！不要离开我。

A: I can’t, Tim. I hope someday you can find some way to understand that I’m only doing this for you. I don’t really want to go, but deep in my heart, I know this is the kindest thing to do.  

A：我做不到，蒂姆。我希望有一天你能够明白，我这样做都是为了你。我其实并不想离开，但在内心深处，我知道分手才是对你最仁慈的选择。

B: Laura...  

B：劳拉……

A: Here are your keys. I’ll send my sister to pick up the rest of my things next week. I’m sorry, Tim. I wish you all the best, and I hope that one day we can meet again. I’ll always love you. Goodbye.  

A：这是你的钥匙。下周我会让妹妹来取走剩下的东西。对不起，蒂姆。祝你今后一切顺利，也希望有一天我们还能再次见面。我会永远爱你。再见。

Oh, come on. And excuse like that is tantamount to lying.

哦，得了吧，这样的借口，简直就是在撒谎。

Chapter 0129 Presidential Speech II 总统演讲（二）

We now stand 10 years past the midpoint of a century that has witnessed four major wars among great nations.

我们现在站在一个世纪的中点之后10年，这个世纪已经见证了强国之间的四次重大战争。

Three of these involved our own country.

其中三起事件，涉及我们自己的国家。

Despite the carnage of these conflicts, America is today the strongest, the most influential, and the most productive nation in the world.

尽管这些冲突带来了巨大的破坏，美国如今仍是世界上最强大、最具影响力、生产力最高的国家。

We are understandably proud of this preeminence. Yet we realize that America's leadership and prestige depend not merely upon our unmatched material progress riches and military strength.

我们理所当然地为这种卓越地位感到自豪，然而，我们也意识到，美国的领导地位和威望，不仅仅依赖于我们无与伦比的物质进步、财富和军事实力。

But on how we use our power in the interests of world peace and human betterment.

但关键在于，我们如何运用自身的力量，去促进世界和平，推动人类福祉的提升。

Throughout America's adventure and free government, such basic purposes have been to keep the peace, to foster progress in human achievement,

在美国的冒险与自由政府历程中，这些基本目标始终是维护和平，促进人类成就的进步，

and to enhance liberty, dignity, and integrity among peoples and among nations.

并且，要在人民之间，以及国家之间，增强自由，尊严，与完整。

We pray that peoples of all faiths, all races, all nations, may have their great human needs satisfied,

我们祈祷，所有信仰、所有种族、所有国家的人们，都能满足他们伟大的人类需求，

that those now denied opportunity shall come to enjoy it to the full.

那些现在被剥夺机会的人，将会充分享受到它。

That all who yearn for freedom may experience its spiritual blessings,

所有渴望自由的人，愿他们都能体验到自由所带来的精神祝福，

that those who have freedom will understand also its heavy responsibilities, that all who are insensitive to the needs of others will learn charity,

那些拥有自由的人，也会理解它所承载的沉重责任，所有对他人需求漠不关心的人，将学会慈善。

that the scourges of poverty, disease and ignorance will be made to disappear from the earth,

贫困、疾病和无知的祸害，将从地球上消失。

and that in the goodness of time, all peoples will come to live together in a peace guaranteed by the binding force of mutual respect and love.

在美好的时光里，所有民族都将共同生活，这种和平由相互尊重和爱的纽带力量所保障。

Now, on Friday noon, I am to become a private citizen.

现在，在星期五中午，我将成为一名普通公民。

I am proud to do so. I look forward to it. Thank you and good night.

我为此感到自豪，我期待着它，谢谢，晚安。

Chapter 136  Gambling	赌博

Are you serious?

你是认真的吗？

Gambling is a vice-industry built on deception and fed by the intentional exploitation of human weakness for the sole purpose of monetary gain.

赌博是一种建立在欺骗之上的恶习产业，它通过有意利用人性的弱点来滋养，其唯一目的就是牟取金钱利益。

But discuss this, me.

它是如何利用人们的？

It ruins marriages, destroys families and bankrupts communities.

它毁掉婚姻，破坏家庭，让社区破产。

Once you're addicted, it's very difficult to stop.

一旦上瘾，就很难戒掉。

People have lost their houses, cars, and have been left out on the street after becoming addicted.

人们失去了房子，失去了汽车，在成瘾后，被遗弃在街头。

Secondly, it exploits because men become addicted to gambling most often because of the action and risk.

其次，它利用了男性对赌博的成瘾，这通常是因为他们沉迷于其中的刺激和风险。

Women gamble to escape and senior citizens will start gambling for social interaction.

女性赌博是为了逃避现实，老年人开始赌博则是为了社交互动。

Underage gamblers often start gambling on sports with friends and then illegal bookies.

Chapter 141 - E0144 - Capital Punishment




Do you think we should be tougher on crime?

你认为，我们应该对犯罪采取更严厉的措施吗？

Well, it depends on what you mean.

嗯，这取决于你的意思。

For example, we could bring back the death penalty for murder,

例如，我们可以恢复对谋杀罪的死刑，

give longer prison sentences for lesser offenses,

对较轻的罪行，判处更长的监禁刑期，

and lock up juvenile offenders.

并且关押青少年罪犯。

Do sound like really draconian measures.

听起来确实像是非常严厉的措施。

Firstly, what do you do about miscarriages of justice if you've already put innocent people to death?

首先，如果你已经处决了无辜的人，你该如何处理这些冤案？

You'd only use capital punishment if you're absolutely sure that you'd convicted the right person.

只有在绝对确定，已经定罪了正确的人，才会使用死刑。

But there've been many cases of wrongful conviction where people have been imprisoned for many years.

但是，有很多错判的案例，这些人被关押了很多年。

The authorities were sure at the time, but later it was shown that the evidence was unreliable.

当局当时很确定，但后来证明，证据并不可靠。

In some cases, it had been fabricated by the police.

在某些情况下，这是由警方捏造的。

Well, no system of justice can be perfect, but surely there's a good case for longer prison sentences to deter serious crime.

嗯，没有任何司法系统是完美的，但肯定有充分的理由支持延长刑期，以威慑严重犯罪。

I doubt whether they could act as an effective deterrent all the detection rate is so low.

我怀疑他们是否能起到有效的威慑作用，因为检测率实在太低了。

The best way to prevent crime is to convince people who committed that they're going to be caught.

预防犯罪的最佳方法，是让那些犯罪的人相信，他们一定会被抓住。

It doesn't make sense to divert all your resources into the prison system.

把所有资源都投入到监狱系统中，是没有意义的。

But if you detect more crime, you still need prisons.

但如果你侦测到更多犯罪，你仍然需要监狱。

In my reckoning, if we could lock up more juvenile criminals, they'd learn that they can't get away with it.

在我看来，如果我们能关押更多的少年犯，他们就会明白，自己无法逃脱惩罚。

Soft sentences will merely encourage them to do it again.

轻判只会鼓励他们再次犯罪。

Yes, but remember that prisons are often schools for criminals.

是的，但请记住，监狱往往是罪犯的学校。

To remove crime from society, you really have to tackle its causes.

要从社会中消除犯罪，你确实需要解决其根源。

Well, if I were president, I would impose tougher laws and punishment.

那么，如果我是总统，我会实施更严格的法律和惩罚。

I would have a peaceful society based on fear of punishment, not consciousness of doing the right thing.

我希望建立一个，基于对惩罚的恐惧，而非基于做正确事情的意识，的和平社会。

You sound like a dictator.

你听起来像个独裁者。

Well, if it works, why not?

那么，如果它有效，为什么不呢？

Chapter 169 - C0173 - Preparing a Budget

最近经济不景气，我丈夫的生意受到了影响，现在我们需要一些建议，关于如何制定家庭预算。

I see. Well, you have come to the right place.

Yes, that makes sense.

是的，这样说得通。

其次，我需要你带上过去两到三个月的所有收据。这样，我们就能确定你的平均支出，看看你在哪个类别上花钱最多。

Usually, your fixed costs are higher and we can't do much about that. But we can usually trim your variable costs such as entertainment or clothing.

通常，你的固定成本较高，我们对此无能为力。但是，我们通常可以削减你的可变成本，比如娱乐或服装。

太好了，我们就这么办吧。现在，我们请你吃顿美味的晚餐，怎么样？

That's another thing. If you really want to stop spending so much money, throw away at least half of your credit cards.

这是另一件事。如果你真的想停止花那么多钱，至少扔掉一半的信用卡。

Which highlights the idea of the business in two pages or less

Well, that seems easy enough

What they are and how they're different from competitors

To how many children on average the population has

Chapter 187 - C0191 - Daylight Savings Time




Did you set your clock forward for daylight savings time?

你为了夏令时，把时钟调快了吗？

What? Why do we have to do that?

什么？我们为什么必须那样做？

Well, at the start of the spring we usually have more daylight in the mornings and lessen the afternoon.

嗯，在春天开始的时候，我们通常在早晨会有更多的日光，而下午则会减少。

This is basically due to our position on the planet and the rotation of the earth.

这基本上是由于我们在地球上的位置，以及地球的自转。

In any case, to take better advantage of the daylight available, we compensate by moving our clocks forward one hour.

无论如何，为了更好地利用可用的日光，我们通过将时钟向前拨一小时来进行补偿。

I see, that's convenient.

我明白了，这确实很方便。

I never understood things like this, such as GMT.

我从来都不理解这类事情，比如格林尼治标准时间。

I never know what time zone we are in or when to change my clock.

我从来不知道我们处于哪个时区，也不知道什么时候该调整我的时钟。

That just stands for Grinnich meantime.

那只是格林尼治时间的缩写。

Here in California, we're in Pacific Standard Time.

在加利福尼亚这里，我们处于太平洋标准时间。

That is 8 times zones west of Grinnich.

那是在格林尼治以西，跨越了8个时区。

Remember when we were in Beijing?

还记得，我们在北京的时候吗？

Well, then we were in China Standard Time and that's 8 times zones east of Grinnich.

那么，我们当时处于中国标准时间，这个时间比格林尼治时间早了8个时区。

That's why it was so weird traveling from Beijing to LA because of the huge time difference.

这就是为什么从北京到洛杉矶的旅行会如此奇怪，因为时差太大了。

Even though we left Beijing at noon and flew for more than 8 hours, we still arrived in LA the same day at noon.

虽然我们中午从北京出发，飞行了超过8个小时，但我们仍然在同一天中午抵达了洛杉矶。

It's like we went back in time.

就像我们回到了过去。

Chapter 194 - C0198 - Applying CPR

.

英播客网。

I need a volunteer.

我需要一名志愿者。

Me. Me.

我。我。

Alright, come here and lay flat on your back.

好的，过来这里，平躺下来。

Let's suppose this young woman has stopped breathing.

让我们假设，这位年轻女子已经停止了呼吸。

We must lift the person's chin so that we clear a pathway for air to get into the lungs.

我们必须抬起这个人的下巴，这样才能清理出一条通道，让空气进入肺部。

Then, we place our mouth over the other person's mouth and blow air two or three times like this.

然后，我们将嘴对准对方的嘴，像这样吹气两到三次。

Wait, what are you doing? I'm a married woman. You can't just try to kiss me like that.

等等，你在做什么？我可是已婚女性，你不能就这样试图亲我。

Ma'am, I'm not trying to kiss you. I'm trying to demonstrate how to apply CPR in the case of an emergency.

女士，我并不是想要亲吻您，我只是在演示，如何在紧急情况下，进行心肺复苏术的操作。

Well, okay. Oh, no French kissing.

好吧，明白了。哦，不要法式接吻。

As I was saying, we blow air through the mouth in this manner.

正如我所说，我们以这种方式，通过嘴巴吹气。

Once this is done, we must try to get the heart going in.

一旦完成这一步，我们必须尝试让心脏重新跳动。

To do this, we place our hands over the person's chest and press down firmly two or three times.

为此，我们将双手放在对方的胸部上方，用力按压两到三次。

Wait, what are you doing? You can't just kiss me then go for second base.

等等，你在做什么？你不能刚亲了我，就马上更进一步。

你是大块头吗？你在交易什么？货币。货币。比如欧元、美元、英镑和卢比？这叫外汇。外汇交易。它的好处是，我不需要投入大量资金。

I put in a margin deposit in the night can buy and sell up to a hundred times that much. I don't understand. You're buying and selling money? You got it. Just last night, I made 150 US dollars. Last night?

我在晚上存入了一笔保证金，可以买卖多达一百倍的资金。我不明白，你在买卖货币吗？你说对了，就在昨晚，我赚了150美元。昨晚？

Yeah, it's a 24 hour market. I bought some remin beer earlier at a low-asking price, but last night it appreciated drastically. So I made a split second decision and sold all of my RMB at an amazing bid.

是的，这是一个24小时的市场。我之前以较低的报价买入了一些人民币，但昨晚它大幅升值。于是，我迅速做出决定，以极好的报价卖出了所有的人民币。

I've also done some trading with Swiss francs and australian dollars and Hong Kong dollars. I've made some good profits, but I've also suffered some losses.

我还做过一些瑞士法郎、澳元和港元的交易。我赚到了一些不错的利润，但也遭受了一些损失。

Depends on a lot of factors, just like any other market. In total, I've made about 500 US dollars in the past few months. You're kidding! I'm on. Where do I sign up?

这取决于很多因素，就像其他市场一样。总的来说，我在过去几个月里赚了大约500美元。你在开玩笑吧！我加入。我该在哪里报名？

Chapter 265 - C0269 - Marketing Plan




Okay everyone let's begin. I call you here today to evaluate our marketing strategy during this recession.

好的，大家，我们开始吧。今天叫大家来，是为了评估一下，在这次经济衰退期间，我们的营销策略。

I wanted to re-emphasize our corporate mission of aiming to give our customers the best coffee and service in a clean and welcoming atmosphere.

我想再次强调，我们的企业使命是，在干净、舒适的氛围中，为顾客提供最好的咖啡和服务。

Several other shops have been reducing their prices for the coffees and they are drawing in more customers. Why aren't we doing the same thing?

其他几家商店已经在降低咖啡的价格，他们吸引了更多的顾客。为什么我们不采取同样的做法呢？

I know that recent sales have been slow, but we are not going to reduce our prices to the level of our competitors.

我知道，最近的销售情况比较缓慢，但我们不会把价格降到竞争对手的水平。

We offer a superior product and our focus is on long-term growth rather than short-term sales.

我们提供优质的产品，我们的重点在于长期增长，而非短期销售。

If we lower our prices we run the risk of devaluing our product.

如果我们降低价格，我们就有可能贬低产品的价值。

Customers don't care about coffee anymore. The only care about price.

顾客不再关心咖啡了，他们只在乎价格。

I disagree. Highly discerning customers know that our coffee is far better than the coffee you buy at the other places.

我不同意。非常挑剔的顾客都知道，我们的咖啡，远远好于你在其他地方买的咖啡。

Our coffee beans are artisan-roasted and we use state-of-the-art equipment to brew our coffees.

我们的咖啡豆采用手工烘焙，并使用最先进的设备来冲泡咖啡。

When you compare the coffees side by side, our coffee wins the taste test every time.

当你把咖啡放在一起比较时，我们的咖啡每次都能赢得口味测试。

We have never sought to appeal to the mass market with cheap coffee drinks, and we will not do so now.

我们从未试图以廉价的咖啡饮品吸引大众市场，现在也不会这样做。

That's true. We've certainly achieved top of minor awareness when it comes to the best tasting brews.

确实如此，在最佳口感的酿造方面，我们无疑已经达到了次要认知的顶端。

And it's important to distinguish ourselves from the competitors.

重要的是，我们要将自己与竞争对手区分开来。

I think the main question is, how can we show our appreciation to our customers?

我认为，主要的问题是，我们该如何表达对客户的感激之情？

That's the main question I would like to discuss today.

这就是我今天想讨论的主要问题。

Money is tight for everyone these days.

如今，每个人的手头都不宽裕。

So even our most loyal customers may be reconsidering the money they pay for their morning coffee.

所以，即使是我们最忠实的顾客，也可能在重新考虑，他们为早晨那杯咖啡所支付的费用。

Since the superiority of our coffee beans is one of our core competencies, why don't we sell the beans for people to brew coffee at home?

既然我们咖啡豆的优越性，是我们的核心竞争力之一，那我们为什么不直接卖豆子，让人们在家自己冲泡咖啡呢？

That could definitely be a way we could expand our company, but would we be undermining the essence of the company that way?

这确实可能是我们扩展公司的一种方式，但这样做，会不会削弱公司的本质呢？

Let's brainstorm some more ideas and do some research. The customer always comes first and what the customer wants the customer gets.

让我们再集思广益一下，做一些研究。顾客永远是第一位的，顾客想要的，顾客就能得到。

Maybe it's time we started selling coffee beans.

也许，是时候开始卖咖啡豆了。

。

Cheese is usually categorized into four types.

Soft, semi soft, semi hard, and hard.

柔软，半柔软，半硬，和硬。

The designation refers to the amount of moisture in the cheese which directly affects its texture.

这一名称指的是奶酪中的水分含量，它直接影响奶酪的质地。

Making cheese is an ancient practice dating back thousands of years, and the home cheese

制作奶酪是一项古老的技艺，已有数千年的历史，而家庭奶酪制作，

maker can usually find recipes for cheese that fall into any of the four categories.

制作者通常能找到属于这四类中任意一类的奶酪配方。

Soft cheese includes cottage cheese, cream cheese, ricotta, brie, blue, rookfort, mozzarella,

软质奶酪包括，农家干酪，奶油奶酪，意大利乳清干酪，布里干酪，蓝纹奶酪，罗克福尔干酪，马苏里拉奶酪，

meanster, and similar cheese.

梅恩斯特奶酪，以及类似的奶酪。

Cheese cheese is generally pair well with fruit or meats, and can be used as breakfast

奶酪，通常与水果或肉类搭配得很好，可以作为早餐食用。

cheeses in omelet or as pasta filling.

奶酪可以放在煎蛋卷里，或者作为意面的馅料。

They are usually mildly flavored and very high in moisture.

它们通常味道温和，水分含量非常高。

American, Colby, Codak, and similar cheeses are in the semi soft category.

美国的科尔比奶酪，科达克奶酪，以及类似的奶酪，都属于半软质奶酪的类别。

These are slightly stronger in flavor and cover a wide range of uses.

这些在风味上稍微更浓郁一些，并且覆盖了广泛的用途。

Codak cheese, a blend of Colby and Monterey Jack, is one of the most popular.

科达克奶酪，是科尔比和蒙特利杰克的混合品种，是最受欢迎的之一。

This allows the sharper flavor of the Colby to be combined with the milder Jack cheese

这样，科尔比奶酪的浓郁风味，就能与温和的杰克奶酪，完美融合在一起。

and also melts better than plain Colby.

而且，它比普通的科尔比奶酪更容易融化。

Grilled cheese sandwiches often use American cheese and Mexican cheeses, such as Asadero

烤奶酪三明治，通常使用美式奶酪，以及墨西哥奶酪，比如阿萨德罗奶酪。

and Gesso Frisco, are becoming more popular.

以及Gesso Frisco，正变得越来越受欢迎。

Hard cheeses include Parmesan, Romano, Asiago, Swiss, Gruyere, and others.

硬质奶酪包括帕尔马干酪、罗马诺干酪、阿齐亚戈干酪、瑞士干酪、格鲁耶尔干酪等。

Parmesan and Romano are most familiar as the grated powder used to top spaghetti, but

帕尔马干酪和罗马诺干酪，最常见的是作为磨碎的粉末，用来撒在意大利面上。

they are also used as a companion meant for fruit, wine, nuts and other appetizer items.

它们也被用作水果、葡萄酒、坚果以及其他开胃小食的伴侣。

Grilled cheese is a popular sandwich cheese and melts well unlike some other hard cheeses.

烤奶酪是一种受欢迎的三明治奶酪，它融化得很好，不像其他一些硬质奶酪。

It's your federal income tax, and then there's Social Security and Medicare, which are both federal programs

Chapter 333 - C0337 - The Night Before Christmas




And Mama and her curchift and I and my camp had just settled down for a long winter's nap.

妈妈和她的教堂，还有我和我的营地，刚刚安顿下来，准备度过一个漫长的冬眠。

When out on the lawn there arose such a clatter. I sprang from the bed to see what was the matter.

当外面草坪上传来一阵喧闹声，我从床上跳起来，想看看发生了什么事。

Away to the window I flew like a flash, tore open the shutters and threw open a sash.

我飞快地冲向窗户，像闪电一样，猛地拉开百叶窗，迅速推开窗框。

The moon on the breast of the new fallen snow gave the luster of midday to objects below.

新落的雪，胸脯上，月亮，给下面的物体，带来了正午般的光辉。

When what to my wondering eyes should appear but a miniature sleigh and eight tiny reindeer.

当我惊讶的眼睛看到什么时，出现了一辆微型雪橇，还有八只小驯鹿。

With a little old driver so lively and quick I knew in a moment it must be Saint Nick.

有一位年迈的驾车人，他如此活泼敏捷，我立刻就知道，那一定是圣尼克。

More rapid than eagles his corsers they came and he whistled and shouted and called them by name.

比鹰还要快，他的骏马们来了，他吹着口哨，大声呼喊，并一一叫出它们的名字。

Now, Dasher, now dancer, now prancer and fixing, on comet on Cupid, on Donder and Blitzson.

现在，达舍，现在，舞者，现在，跳跃者和修补者，在彗星上，在丘比特上，在顿德和布利茨森上。

To the top of the porch to the top of the wall. Now dash away, dash away, dash away all.

到门廊的顶端，到墙的顶端。现在，快跑吧，快跑吧，全都快跑吧。

As dry leaves that before the wild hurricane fly when they meet with an obstacle mount to the sky.

如同干燥的树叶，在狂野的飓风前飞舞，当它们遇到障碍时，便升向天空。

So up to the house top the corsers they flew with the sleigh's full of toys and Saint Nicholas too.

于是，他们飞上了屋顶，驾着满载玩具的雪橇，还有圣尼古拉斯。

And then in a twinkling I heard on the roof the prancing and powing of each little hoof.

接着，在一瞬间，我听到屋顶上，每只小蹄子欢腾跳跃的声音。

As I drew in my head and was turning around down the chimney Saint Nicholas came with a bound.

当我缩回头，正准备转身离开烟囱时，圣尼古拉斯突然一跃而下。

He was dressed on fur from his head to his toe and his clothes were all tarnished with ashes and suit.

他从头到脚都穿着毛皮，他的衣服全都被灰烬和煤烟弄脏了。

A bundle of toys he had flung on his back and he looked like a pedlar just opening his pack.

他把一捆玩具甩在背上，看起来像个小贩，正打开他的包裹。

His eyes, how they twinkled, his dimples, how merry.

他的眼睛，多么闪烁，他的酒窝，多么欢快。

His cheeks were like roses, his nose like a cherry, his drool little mouth was drawn up in a bow and the beard of his chin was as white as the snow.

他的脸颊像玫瑰一样红润，他的鼻子像樱桃一样小巧，他那流着口水的小嘴弯成了弓形，他下巴上的胡子像雪一样白。

The stump of a pipe he held tight in his teeth and the smoke and encircled his head like a reef.

他紧紧咬住烟斗的残端，烟雾缭绕，像礁石一样环绕着他的头。

He had a broad face in the little round belly that shook when he laughed like a bowl full of jelly.

他有一张宽大的脸，小小的圆肚子，笑起来时，肚子像装满果冻的碗一样抖动。

He was chubby and plump, a rattle jolly elf and I laughed when I saw him in spite of myself.

他胖乎乎的，是个欢快的小精灵，我看到他时，忍不住笑了。

A wink of his eye and a twist of his head soon gave me to know I had nothing to dread.

他眨了一下眼，又扭了扭头，我很快就明白了，我没什么好怕的。

He spoke not a word but when straight to work and filled all the stockings then turned with a jerk.

他一言不发，直接开始工作，把所有的袜子都装满，然后猛地转身。

And laying his finger aside of his nose and giving a nod up the chimney he rose.

他把手指放在鼻子旁边，朝烟囱点了点头，然后站了起来。

He sprang to his sleigh to his team gave a whistle and the way they all flew like the down of a whistle.

他跳上雪橇，向他的队伍吹了一声口哨，他们便像一阵风一样飞驰而去。

But I heard an exclaim air as you drove out of sight.

但我听到一声惊呼，当你驶出视线时。

Happy Christmas to all and to all a good night!

祝大家圣诞快乐，愿所有人度过一个美好的夜晚！

。

好的，也许我们可以从街边的餐厅点一份汤，和一份沙拉。

I was thinking of getting a hamburger, fries, and a chocolate sundae.

我在考虑买一个汉堡，薯条，还有一个巧克力圣代。

You eat too much junk food. That sort of stuff clogs up your arteries and is very high in cholesterol.

你吃了太多垃圾食品，那些东西会堵塞你的动脉，而且胆固醇含量非常高。

Well, I never seen the game way, so I don't mind.

好吧，我从未见过这种玩法，所以我不介意。

It's not only about getting fatter-not, it's about being healthy.

这不仅仅是关于变胖，不，这是关于保持健康。

You could really have some health problems later on.

你以后真的可能会遇到一些健康问题。

How about pizza? Maybe some fried chicken. Better yet. Let's order some hot dogs.

披萨怎么样？也许来点炸鸡。更好的选择是，我们点些热狗吧。

You're a lost cause.

你是个没救的人。

Chapter 358 - C0362 - What Mood Are You In


Are you okay? You see a bit anxious?

你还好吗？你看起来有点焦虑？

Yeah, I'm okay. I've been having a lot of mood swings lately.

嗯，我还好，最近情绪波动挺大的。

I think it has to do with the pills my doctor prescribed that are causing chaos on my hormones.

我认为，这与我医生开的药有关，这些药正在扰乱我的荷尔蒙。

So you mean you feel ecstatic one minute and then blew the next?

所以你是说，你前一分钟还感到欣喜若狂，后一分钟就情绪失控了？

Yeah, it's weird. For example, just this morning I was feeling detached and lonely.

是啊，这很奇怪。比如，就在今天早上，我还感到疏离和孤独。

You know there was really no reason to feel that way.

你知道，其实没有理由那样感觉。

Well, maybe your mood will swing positively and you will feel confident, brave and hopeful.

那么，也许你的心情会变得积极，你会感到自信、勇敢和充满希望。

I hope you're right.

我希望你是对的。

Chapter 359 - C0363 - Living With Your Parents

Why did your girlfriend break up with you?

你女朋友为什么要和你分手？

I don't know.

我不知道.

She said she was tired of me not manning up and being more independent.

她说，她厌倦了我没有担当，不够独立。

Which I think is all much a crap.

我觉得，这些都是胡扯。

Well, you still live with your parents, so she does have a point.

嗯，你还和父母住在一起，所以她确实有道理。

What do you mean a lot of people live with their parents,

你是说，很多人和父母住在一起，

especially when rent is so expensive in the slump in the economy?

尤其是在经济低迷时期，房租如此昂贵的情况下？

Yeah, but you're almost 40.

是啊，但你已经快40岁了。

Plus, you make good money, so there's really no excuse.

而且，你赚得不少，所以真的没有借口。

It's just simply because you're a mama's boy.

这仅仅是因为，你是个妈宝男。

Whatever, dude, I have everything I need.

随便吧，伙计，我什么都不缺。

Why would I move out?

我为什么要搬出去呢？

Have a great roof over my head.

我有一个很好的屋顶，为我遮风挡雨。

My mom does my laundry and cooks for me.

我妈妈给我洗衣服，还为我做饭。

What else could a guy ask for?

一个男人还能奢求什么呢？

Let's agree to disagree.

让我们同意保留各自的不同意见。

Chapter 360 - C0364 - Hiring Help

A: Can you help me write a newspaper ad?  
A：你能帮我写一则报纸广告吗？

B: Sure, what are you looking to buy or sell?  
B：当然可以，你想买什么还是卖什么？

A: Actually, I want to hire someone to help me around the house.  
A：其实，我想雇个人帮我做些家务。

B: Oh, you want to get a maid?  
B：哦，你是想雇一位女佣吗？

A: Well, I think it’s better if you call her a cleaning lady or domestic help.  
A：嗯，我觉得称她为清洁工或家政人员更合适。

B: OK, so what do you want her to do?  
B：好的，那你想让她做些什么呢？

A: Well, let’s see. I want her to come in three times a week for a couple of hours to clean the kitchen, bathroom and maybe do some cooking.  
A：嗯，让我想想。我希望她每周来三次，每次工作两三个小时，打扫厨房和卫生间，也可以顺便做做饭。

B: Got it. And how much do you offer per month?  
B：明白了。那你每个月打算付多少工资呢？

A: I would pay her hourly. I don’t know what the going rate is, though.  
A：我打算按小时付费，不过我不知道现在通常的时薪是多少。


Chapter 361 - C0365 - Household Chores


A: Kevin, what is this mess? It looks like a pigsty in here! Clean this up!  
A：凯文，这里怎么乱成这样？简直像个猪圈！快把这里收拾干净！

B: OK, Dad. I will do it in a minute. Let me just finish this level of the game.  
B：好的，爸爸，我一会儿就收拾。先让我打完这一关。

A: No, I said now! Plus, you are grounded. You’re not allowed to play video games. I want you to make your bed, do the laundry, and then come downstairs and sweep the floors.  
A：不行，我说的是现在！而且你正在被禁足，不许玩电子游戏。我要你先整理床铺、洗衣服，然后下楼扫地。

B: That’s so unfair!  
B：这太不公平了！

A: You have to pull your weight around here, young man. My house, my rules.  
A：小伙子，你也得为这个家尽一份力。我的家，就得按我的规矩来。

B: But I already mopped the floors, dusted the furniture, and vacuumed the rugs!  
B：可我已经拖过地、擦过家具，还用吸尘器清理过地毯了！

A: That’s great, but you still have work to do, so get to it.  
A：那很好，但你还有活儿要干，所以赶快去做吧。
